# System Architect Original Certification-Style Q&A

These are original practice questions for preparation. They are not copied exam dumps.

## Foundation

### Q1. [best practice] How should a Salesforce professional handle deployment for multi-system enterprise Salesforce architecture?

**Answer:** For System Architect, the correct approach is to connect deployment to the business requirement, the Salesforce data model, security, limits, and operational support. In a multi-system enterprise Salesforce architecture scenario, start by clarifying volume, users, permissions, integration boundaries, and failure handling. A strong implementation answer names the chosen Salesforce feature, why alternatives were rejected, how it is tested, and how admins or support teams will operate it after go-live.

**Interview Follow-Up:** Describe one risk, one test case, and one monitoring signal for this answer.

### Q2. [support incident] How should a Salesforce professional handle data architecture for multi-system enterprise Salesforce architecture?

**Answer:** For System Architect, the correct approach is to connect data architecture to the business requirement, the Salesforce data model, security, limits, and operational support. In a multi-system enterprise Salesforce architecture scenario, start by clarifying volume, users, permissions, integration boundaries, and failure handling.
